    spends: SpendPermissionDetail[];
    permissionId: Hex;
    chainId: Hex;
};

// @public
export type WalletRevokePermissionsRequest = {
    method: 'wallet_revokePermissions';
    params: [
        {
        address?: Address_2;
        id: Hex;
        capabilities?: RequestCapabilities;
    }
    ];
};

// @public
export interface WalletSignUIRequest extends BaseUIRequest {
    // (undocumented)
    data: {
        address: Address;
        chainId?: number;
        request: PersonalSignRequestData | TypedDataRequestData;
    };
    // (undocumented)
    type: 'wallet_sign';
}
